<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The user is building a comprehensive academic management system, EduNet.

Initially, the project included a Registro Auxiliar (Gradebook), Consolidado de Notas (Grade Report), and a manual membership renewal system.

During this session, the user requested and the agent implemented a series of new features and bug fixes:
1.  **UI/UX Improvements:**
    -   Fixed a mobile sidebar overlay bug.
    -   Implemented placeholder-row views for the Consolidado de Notas and Registro Auxiliar when a section has no students, improving the user experience by avoiding empty screens.
2.  **Support Panel Overhaul:**
    -   Fixed the X button for un-assigning schools, and later removed the entire assign/unassign feature per user request.
    -   Rebuilt the Finanzas (Finance) page with advanced filtering (by day, month, year, date range), a detailed transaction table, and summary charts.
    -   Added the ability to delete individual payments from the Finance page.
    -   Implemented a new flow to create schools directly from the support panel. This was refined to:
        -   Not seed demo data.
        -   Automatically register the creation fee in the Finance module.
        -   Pull the fee amount from a global Precios configuration instead of a manual input field.
    -   Updated the school deletion process to also remove all associated financial records.
3.  **Payment and Renewal Flow:**
    -   Enhanced the manual membership renewal flow to require a support user to validate an 8-digit transaction code provided by the client.
    -   Added a conditional Pagar button on school cards. This button only appears if a payment for the school's active period is missing (e.g., deleted from the Finance page), allowing support to restore it.
4.  **Bug Fixes & Investigations:**
    -   Resolved a critical bug preventing image uploads by fixing a missing  in a backend route (), which was introduced during a previous agent's automated refactoring.
    -   Investigated why the user couldn't find the Replicar Asignaturas (Replicate Subjects) feature, clarifying that it was already implemented and explaining its conditional visibility.
    -   The session ended while investigating a data inconsistency where the Asignaturas page and the Asignar Docente modal show different subject lists for the same section.

**User's preferred language**: Spanish

**what currently exists?**
The application is a full-stack system using React, FastAPI, and MongoDB.
-   **Backend:** The codebase is modular, with routes separated into files like , , , , and . It features a robust database auto-discovery mechanism, extensive support panel endpoints for managing schools and finances, and Cloudinary integration for image uploads.
-   **Frontend:** The UI includes role-based dashboards for School Owners and Support staff. Significant work was done on the support panel pages (, ), and academic report pages (, ) to handle empty states gracefully. Modals are used extensively for user input workflows.

**Last working item**:
-   Last item agent was working: Investigating a data inconsistency issue. The user reported that for a specific section (3 años - Sección UNICA), the main Asignaturas page shows only one subject, while the Asignar Docente (Assign Teacher) modal's dropdown shows multiple subjects. This suggests the two UI components are being fed by backend APIs with different filtering logic.
-   Status: **IN PROGRESS**

-   **Issue 1: Subject List Inconsistency (P0)**
    -   **Description:** The Asignaturas page () displays a different (smaller) number of subjects for a section compared to the list shown in the Asignar Docente modal for the exact same section.
    -   **Attempted fixes:** The agent acknowledged the issue and was about to begin investigation by examining the relevant backend and frontend files.
    -   **Next debug checklist:**
        1.  Identify the API endpoint and its filtering logic used by the  page to fetch the list of subjects.
        2.  Identify the API endpoint and its filtering logic used by the Asignar Docente modal to populate its subject dropdown.
        3.  Compare the database queries in both backend endpoints (likely within ).
        4.  Unify the logic to ensure both endpoints correctly filter subjects by , , , and .
    -   **Why fix this issue and what will be achieved with the fix?** To resolve a confusing data discrepancy for the user and ensure consistent information is displayed across the platform.
    -   **Status:** IN PROGRESS
    -   **Is recurring issue?** N
    -   **Should Test frontend/backend/both after fix?** Both
    -   **Blocked on other issue:** None

-   **Issue 2: Production Database is Missing (CRITICAL P0)**
    -   **Description:** An infrastructure-level issue where the user's primary production database (containing key schools like , ) is not accessible to the application. Debugging confirmed the data is not on the connected MongoDB cluster.
    -   **Next debug checklist:** This cannot be fixed with code. The user has been instructed to work with Emergent support for a data backup/restore. The agent must not attempt further code fixes for this.
    -   **Why fix this issue and what will be achieved with the fix?** The application is non-functional in production without its real data.
    -   **Status:** BLOCKED
    -   **Is recurring issue?** N
    -   **Should Test frontend/backend/both after fix?** Both
    -   **Blocked on other issue:** Blocked on Emergent support action.

**Code Architecture**


**Key Technical Concepts**
-   **Conditional UI Rendering:** Logic was heavily used to show/hide UI elements based on data state (e.g., placeholder rows for empty tables, the conditional Pagar button).
-   **Data Consistency Debugging:** The current issue involves diagnosing why two different parts of the UI show different data, pointing to inconsistent backend logic.
-   **Modal-Driven Workflows:** Extensive use of modals for creating schools, renewing memberships, and restoring payments.
